<script>
var that=this;

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
	
	$("#form-login-content input").on("click", function(){
		return !($("#login-section").hasClass("disabled") || $("#login-recovery-section").hasClass("disabled"))
	})

	function transition(type){
		if (type==="in"){
			$("#login-section").addClass("disabled")
			$("#login-recovery-section").addClass("disabled")
			$("#login-form").css("opacity",0.5)
		} else {
			$("#login-section").removeClass("disabled")
			$("#login-recovery-section").removeClass("disabled")
			$("#login-form").css("opacity",1)
		}
	}

	/* Start login */
	that.submitFunc = function () {
		
		if ($("#login-section").hasClass("disabled")){
			return false;
		}

		var wrong_password_msg = 'El nombre de usuario/email o la contraseña son incorrectos';
		var session_limit_msg = 'Ha superado el número de sesiones simultáneas contratadas.';
		var server_error_msg = 'Error del servidor. Vuelve a intentarlo pasados unos minutos.'

		transition("in")
		$('.error').hide();

		$.ajax({
			type: 'POST',
			url: "https://vlex.com/session?t=" +(new Date().getTime()),
			data: {
				model: {
					login: $('#input_email').val(),
					password: $('#input_password').val()
				}
			},
			xhrFields: {
				withCredentials: true
			},
			success: function () {
				window.location = "https://app.vlex.com";
				transition("out")
			},
			error: function (jqXHR) {
				var message;
				if (jqXHR.status === 403 || jqXHR.status === 401 ) {
					if (JSON.parse(jqXHR.responseText).error === "Authentication failed") {
						message = wrong_password_msg;
					} else {
						message = session_limit_msg;
					}
				} else {
					message = server_error_msg;
				}

				$('#login-section .error').text(message).show();
				transition("out")
			}
		});

		return false;

	}
	/* End login */

	/*Start password recovery */
	

	that.showPassRecovery = function(){
		transition("in")
		$(".title.login").hide()
		$(".title.password-recovery").show()
		$("#login-section").hide()
		
		$("#input_send").attr("type","button")
		$("#send-pass-recovery").attr("type","submit")

		$("#login-recovery-section").show()
		$("#ask-for-password-section").show()
		$("#send-pass-recovery").show()
		transition("out")
		return false;
	}

	that.sendPassRecovery = function(e){
		e.preventDefault()
		e.stopPropagation()

		if ($("#login-recovery-section").hasClass("disabled")){
			return false;
		}

		if (!/@/.test($("#pass_recover_input_email").val())){
			$("#login-recovery-section").addClass("error-block")
			$(".error").text("Email incorrecto. Por favor introduce uno válido").show()
			return false;
		}

		$("#login-recovery-section").removeClass("error-block")
		$(".msgs p").hide()
		transition("in")
		
		$.ajax({
			'type': 'POST',
			'url':  'https://app.vlex.com/account/password_reset',
			'data': {'login' : $("#pass_recover_input_email").val() },
			success: function(resp){
				$("#ask-for-password-section").hide()
				$("#send-pass-recovery").hide()
				$(".msgs").show()
				$(".success").text("En breve le llegará un correo a " + $("#pass_recover_input_email").val() + " para restablecer su contraseña").show()
				$("#pass_recover_input_email").val("")
				transition("out")
			},
	  		error: function(resp) {
				$(".msgs").show()
				$("#login-recovery-section").addClass("error-block")
				$(".error").text("Ha ocurrido un error. Por favor, comprueba que el email es correcto o inténtalo más tarde").show()
				transition("out")
	  		}
	  	})
	}

	that.backToLogin = function(){
		transition("in")
		$('.error').text("")
		$("#login-recovery-section").removeClass("error-block")
		$(".msgs p").hide()
		$(".title.password-recovery").hide()
		$(".title.login").show()
		
		$("#send-pass-recovery").attr("type","button")
		$("#input_send").attr("type","submit")

		$("#login-recovery-section").hide()
		$("#login-section").show()
		transition("out")

		return false;
	}
	/*End password recovery */
})
</script></div><div class="mfp-hide white-popup-block" id="header-sign-up-content">

<script>
     var that=this;
     function validaSignup(form, field) {
          var handleError = function(field, message) {
               var dropdownField = $(field).hasClass("tmp-custom-dropdown")

               if (dropdownField){
                    $(field).closest("div.field-group-block").find(".validationError").remove();
                    $(field).closest("div.field-group-block").addClass("error");
               } else {
                    $(field).closest("div").find(".validationError").remove();
                    $(field).closest("div").addClass("error");
               }
               
               if (message) {
                    if (dropdownField){
                         $(field).closest(".field-group").parent().append("<p class='error validationError'>" + message +"</p>");
                    } else {
                         $(field).closest("div").append("<p class='validationError'>" + message +"</p>");
                    }
               }
          }

          function validateSpainPhone(field){
               var esRegExPattern = /^(34|0034)?[9|6|8|7]\d{8}$/
               return field.value && field.value.replace(/\D+/g, "").match(esRegExPattern)
          }

          function validateGeneralPhone(field){
               var phoneRegex = /^[0-9]{7,25}$/
               return field.value && field.value.replace(/\D+/g, "").match(phoneRegex)
          }

          that.sanitizePhoneNumber = function(field) {
               field = $(field);
               field.val(field.val().replace(/\D+/g, ""))
               return true;
          }

          that.mapTitle = function(val) {
               var title = { 'default' : 'default', '1' : "Abogado", '17' : "Administrador de Empresas", '15' : "Asesor/Gestor", '2' : "Bibliotecario / Documentalista", '12' : "Consulta Particular", '13' : "Contador / Contable", '18' : "Contador Público", '19' : "Contador Público/Revisor Fiscal", '20' : "Docente", '7' : "Estudiante", '16' : "Estudiante de Derecho", '21' : "Financiero", '9' : "Investigador", '22' : "Investigador Académico", '3' : "Notario", '23' : "Otra profesión distinta de las anteriores", '8' : "Otra Profesión Legal", '14' : "Otra profesión relacionada con el derecho", '6' : "Otro", '10' : "Personal Administración Pública", '4' : "Procurador", '11' : "Profesional no relacionado con el derecho", '5' : "Profesor" }

               return title[val]
          }

          that.mapIndustry = function(val) {
               var industry = { 'default' : 'default', '1' : 'Inst. Académica con facultad derecho', '2' : 'Despacho profesional > 10 abogados', '3' : 'Despacho profesional < 10 abogados', '4' : 'Asociación de Abogados', '5' : 'Otros', '6' : 'PYME / Autónomo', '7' : 'CT- Autor - Doctrina', '8' : 'CT- Autor - Contratos', '9' : 'CT- Autor - Varios', '10' : 'CT- Vpublishing', '11' : 'Abogado independiente', '12' : 'Inst. Académica sin facultad derecho', '13' : 'Agencia Gubernamental', '14' : 'Gran Empresa', '15' : 'Administración Pública', '16' : 'Despacho', '17' : 'Firma', '18' : 'Fundaciones, ONGs', '19' : 'Notaría', '20' : 'Profesional Independiente', '21' : 'PYME', '22' : 'Sector Académico' }

               return industry[val]
          }

          that.isFieldDisplayed= function(field){
               return $(field) && $(field).parent() && $(field).parent().css("display") !== "none";
          }

          var emptyError = function(field) {
               return !field.value || field.value == field.placeholder
          }
          
          //we use this to detect failed inputs at any moment
          var someFieldFailed = false;
          var emailRegex = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.]+([.][a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+)*[@][a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+([.][a-zA-Z0-9_]+)*[.][a-zA-Z]{2,4}$/i;
          
          // Remove all errors
          if (!field) {
               $("form .validationError").remove();
               $("form input.error").removeClass("error");
          }

          /***************************/
          /* FIELDS ERRORS DETECTION */
          /***************************/

           if (!field || field.id === "cond_generales"){
               if (!form.cond_generales.checked) {
                    //handleError(form.cond_generales, "Debes aceptar las condiciones de uso");
                    alert("Debes aceptar las condiciones de uso")
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          // Email
          if (!field || field.id === "input_email") {
               if (emptyError(form.input_email)) {
                    handleError(form.input_email, "El Email es obligatorio");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               } else if (emailRegex.test(form.input_email.value) == false) {
                    handleError(form.input_email, "El Email no es valido");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Name
          if (form.input_name && (!field || field.id === "input_name")) {
               if (emptyError(form.input_name)) {
                    handleError(form.input_name, "El nombre es obligatorio");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Lastname
          if (form.input_ape && (!field || field.id === "input_ape")) {
               if (emptyError(form.input_ape)) {
                    handleError(form.input_ape, "Los apellidos son obligatorios");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Password
          if (!field || field.id === "input_password") {
              if (emptyError(form.input_password)) {
                  handleError(form.input_password, "La contraseña es obligatoria");
                  someFieldFailed = true;
              } else if (form.input_password.value.length < 4) {
                  handleError(form.input_password, "La contraseña es demasiado corta");
                  someFieldFailed = true;
              }

              if (someFieldFailed){
                  return false;
              }
          }

          //Password repeat  
          if (!field || field.id === "input_password_repeat") {
              if (form.input_password.value != form.input_password_repeat.value) {
                  handleError(form.input_password_repeat, "La contraseña y su confirmación no coinciden");
                  someFieldFailed = true;
              }

              if (someFieldFailed){
                  return false;
              }
          }

          //Phone
          if (!field || field.id === "phone"){
              if (emptyError(form.phone)) {
                   handleError(form.phone, "El teléfono es obligatorio");
                   someFieldFailed = true;
              } else if (!validateGeneralPhone(form.phone)){       
                   handleError(form.phone, "El teléfono es incorrecto");
                   someFieldFailed = true;
              } else if (!field){
                   that.sanitizePhoneNumber(form.phone)
              }
          
              if (someFieldFailed){
                   return false;
              }
          }

          //Job title
          if (!field || field.id === "title_signup"){
               if (form.title_signup.value === "default"){
                    handleError(form.title_signup, "La profesión es obligatoria");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Sub Job Title (industry)
          if (!field || (field.id === "abogados_signup" || field.id === "instituciones_signup" || field.id === "profesionales_signup" || field.id === "administracion_signup") ){
               if (!field){
                    //submit
                    $.each(["#abogados_signup","#instituciones_signup","#profesionales_signup","#administracion_signup"],function(i,el){
                         if (that.isFieldDisplayed($(el)) && $(el).val()==="default"){
                              handleError(el, "El tipo de organización es obligatorio");
                              someFieldFailed = true;
                              return false;
                         }
                    })
               } else {
                    //changing field
                    if (that.isFieldDisplayed(field) && field.value === "default"){
                         handleError(field, "El tipo de organización es obligatorio");
                         someFieldFailed = true;
                    }
                    
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Main jurisdiction content selection
          if (!field || field.id === "input_pais"){
               if (form.input_pais.value === "default"){
                    handleError(form.input_pais, "La jurisdicción principal de interés es obligatoria");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
          }

          return !someFieldFailed;
          /**********************************/
          /* END OF FIELDS ERRORS DETECTION */
          /**********************************/
     }


     //After DOM Content Load
     document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {

          that.show_industry= function(valor){
               //setting the selected value in the hidden form field
               $("#hidden2_signup, #hidden2_request_info").val($("#title_signup, #title_request_info").not(":hidden").find("option[value='"+ $("#title_signup, #title_request_info").not(":hidden").val() +"']").text())
               
               if(valor == '1'){
                    clean_industry();
                    $('#abogados_div_signup, #abogados_div_request_info').css("display", "block");
                    update_hidden1();
               } else if (valor == '7' || valor == '5' || valor == '9' || valor == '2'){
                    clean_industry();
                    $('#instituciones_div_signup, #instituciones_div_request_info').css("display", "block");
                    update_hidden1();
               } else if (valor == '11'){
                    clean_industry();
                    $('#profesionales_div_signup, #profesionales_div_request_info').css("display", "block");
                    update_hidden1();
               } else if (valor == '10'){
                    clean_industry();
                    $('#administracion_div_signup, #administracion_div_request_info').css("display", "block");
                    update_hidden1();
               } else if (valor == '14' || valor == '19'){
                    clean_industry()
                    $("#hidden1_signup").val($('#otros_signup option:first').val())
                    $("#hidden1_request_info").val($('#otros_request_info option:first').val())
               } else if (valor == '16'){
                    clean_industry()
                    $("#hidden1_signup").val($('#estudiantes_signup option:first').val())
                    $("#hidden1_request_info").val($('#estudiantes_request_info option:first').val())
               } else if (valor == '3'){
                    clean_industry()
                    $("#hidden1_signup").val($('#notaria_signup option:first').val())
                    $("#hidden1_request_info").val($('#notaria_request_info option:first').val())
               } else {
                    clean_industry();  
                    $('#hidden1_signup, #hidden1_request_info').val("5");
               }
          }

          function clean_industry(){
               $(".field-group-block.industry").find(".validationError").remove()
               $(".field-group-block.industry").removeClass("error")

               $('#abogados_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#otros_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#estudiantes_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#notaria_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#instituciones_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#profesionales_div_signup').css("display", "none");
               $('#administracion_div_signup').css("display", "none");

               $('#abogados_signup').val("default");
               $('#instituciones_signup').val("default");
               $('#profesionales_signup').val("default");
               $('#administracion_signup').val("default");

               $('#abogados_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#otros_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#estudiantes_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#notaria_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#instituciones_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#profesionales_div_request_info').css("display", "none");
               $('#administracion_div_request_info').css("display", "none");

               $('#abogados_request_info').val("default");
               $('#instituciones_request_info').val("default");
               $('#profesionales_request_info').val("default");
               $('#administracion_request_info').val("default");
               
               $('#hidden1_signup, #hidden1_request_info').val("default");
          }

          function update_hidden1(){
               $('#abogados_signup, #instituciones_signup, #administracion_signup, #profesionales_signup,#abogados_request_info, #instituciones_request_info, #administracion_request_info, #profesionales_request_info').off("change").on("change", function(){
                    $("#hidden1_signup, #hidden1_request_info").val($(this).val());
                    $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").find(".validationError").remove();
                    $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").removeClass("error");
                    that.validaSignup($("#signup-form")[0], this);
               })
          }

     
          $("#signup-form").submit(function(){ 
               if (this && this.phone){
                    that.sanitizePhoneNumber(this.phone)
               }
               
               //setting the industry in the industry hidden form field
               if ($(".industry>div select").not(":hidden").length>0)
                $("#hidden1_signup, #hidden1_request_info").val($(".industry>div select").not(":hidden").val())

               /* if (this && this.title){
                   $(this.hidden2).val(that.mapTitle($(this.title).val()))
               }

               if (this && (this.abogados || this.instituciones || this.profesionales || this.administracion)){
               
                    $.each(["#abogados","#instituciones","#profesionales","#administracion"],function(i,el){
                         if (that.isFieldDisplayed($(el)) && $(el).val()!=="default"){
                              $(this.hidden1).val(that.mapIndustry($(el).val()))
                              return false;
                         }
                    })
               } */
          });


          $("#signup-form input").on("keydown", function() {
               $(this).closest("div").find(".validationError").remove();
               $(this).closest("div").removeClass("error");
          })

          $("#signup-form input").on("focusout", function() {
               that.validaSignup($("#signup-form")[0], this);
          })

          //$("#signup-form select#title").on("change", function() {
          $("#signup-form select.tmp-custom-dropdown").on("change", function() {
               $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").find(".validationError").remove();
               $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").removeClass("error");
               that.validaSignup($("#signup-form")[0], this);
          })

          if ( $("#default_jurisdiction_content") && $("#default_jurisdiction_content").val()){
               $("#input_pais").val($("#default_jurisdiction_content").val())
          }
     })

<script>
     var that=this;
     
     function validaRequestInfo(form, field) {
          var handleError = function(field, message) {
               var dropdownField = $(field).hasClass("tmp-custom-dropdown")

               if (dropdownField){
                    $(field).closest("div.field-group-block").find(".validationError").remove();
                    $(field).closest("div.field-group-block").addClass("error");
               } else {
                    $(field).closest("div").find(".validationError").remove();
                    $(field).closest("div").addClass("error");
               }
               
               if (message) {
                    if (dropdownField){
                         $(field).closest(".field-group").parent().append("<p class='error validationError'>" + message +"</p>");
                    } else {
                         $(field).closest("div").append("<p class='validationError'>" + message +"</p>");
                    }
               }
          }

          function validateSpainPhone(field){
               var esRegExPattern = /^(34|0034)?[9|6|8|7]\d{8}$/
               return field.value && field.value.replace(/\D+/g, "").match(esRegExPattern)
          }

          function validateGeneralPhone(field){
               var phoneRegex = /^[0-9]{7,25}$/
               return field.value && field.value.replace(/\D+/g, "").match(phoneRegex)
          }

          that.sanitizePhoneNumber = function(field) {
               field = $(field);
               field.val(field.val().replace(/\D+/g, ""))
               return true;
          }

          function isFieldDisplayed(field){
               return $(field) && $(field).parent() && $(field).parent().css("display") !== "none";
          }

          var emptyError = function(field) {
               return !field.value || field.value == field.placeholder
          }
          
          //we use this to detect failed inputs at any moment
          var someFieldFailed = false;
          var emailRegex = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.]+([.][a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+)*[@][a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+([.][a-zA-Z0-9_]+)*[.][a-zA-Z]{2,4}$/i;
          
          // Remove all errors
          if (!field) {
               $("form .validationError").remove();
               $("form input.error").removeClass("error");
          }

          /***************************/
          /* FIELDS ERRORS DETECTION */
          /***************************/

          if (!field || field.id === "cond_generales"){
               if (!form.cond_generales.checked) {
                    //handleError(form.cond_generales, "Debes aceptar las condiciones de uso");
                    alert("Debes aceptar las condiciones de uso")
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          // Email
          if (!field || field.id === "input_email") {
               if (emptyError(form.input_email)) {
                    handleError(form.input_email, "El email es obligatorio");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               } else if (emailRegex.test(form.input_email.value) == false) {
                    handleError(form.input_email, "El email no es válido");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Name
          if (form.input_name && (!field || field.id === "input_name")) {
               if (emptyError(form.input_name)) {
                    handleError(form.input_name, "El nombre es obligatorio");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Lastname
          if (form.input_ape && (!field || field.id === "input_ape")) {
               if (emptyError(form.input_ape)) {
                    handleError(form.input_ape, "Los apellidos son obligatorios");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }
               
               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }


          //Phone
          if (!field || field.id === "phone"){
               if (emptyError(form.phone)) {
                    handleError(form.phone, "El teléfono es obligatorio");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               } else if (!validateGeneralPhone(form.phone)){       
                    handleError(form.phone, "El teléfono es incorrecto");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               } else if (!field){
                    that.sanitizePhoneNumber(form.phone)
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Job title
          if (!field || field.id === "title_request_info"){
               if (form.title_request_info.value === "default"){
                    handleError(form.title_request_info, "La profesión es obligatoria");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Sub Job Title (industry)
          if (!field || (field.id === "abogados_request_info" || field.id === "instituciones_request_info" || field.id === "profesionales_request_info" || field.id === "administracion_request_info") ){
               if (!field){
                    //submit
                    $.each(["#abogados_request_info","#instituciones_request_info","#profesionales_request_info","#administracion_request_info"],function(i,el){
                         if (isFieldDisplayed($(el)) && $(el).val()==="default"){
                              handleError(el, "El tipo de organización es obligatorio");
                              someFieldFailed = true;
                              return false;
                         }
                    })
               } else {
                    //changing field
                    if (isFieldDisplayed(field) && field.value === "default"){
                         handleError(field, "El tipo de organización es obligatorio");
                         someFieldFailed = true;
                    }
                    
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          //Country selection
          if (!field || field.id === "input_pais"){
               if (form.input_pais.value === "default"){
                    handleError(form.input_pais, "La selección de país es obligatoria");
                    someFieldFailed = true;
               }

               if (someFieldFailed){
                    return false;
               }
          }

          return !someFieldFailed;
          /**********************************/
          /* END OF FIELDS ERRORS DETECTION */
          /**********************************/
     }


     //After DOM Content Load
     document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
          if ($(".geo_ip_country") && $(".geo_ip_country").text().length>1 && $(".geo_ip_country").text().length<=3){
              $("#request-info-form #input_pais option[value="+$(".geo_ip_country").text()+"]").attr("selected", "selected")
          }
          $("#lead_source_url").val(encodeURI(window.location.href))

     
          $('#request-info-form').submit(function(){ 
               if (this && this.phone){
                    that.sanitizePhoneNumber(this.phone)
               }
          });


          $("#request-info-form input").on("keydown", function() {
               $(this).closest("div").find(".validationError").remove();
               $(this).closest("div").removeClass("error");
          })

          $("#request-info-form input").on("focusout", function() {
               that.validaRequestInfo($("#request-info-form")[0], this);
          })

          //$("#request-info-form select#title").on("change", function() {
          $("#request-info-form select.tmp-custom-dropdown").on("change", function() {
               $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").find(".validationError").remove();
               $(this).closest("div.field-group-block").removeClass("error");
               that.validaRequestInfo($("#request-info-form")[0], this);
          })
     })

</script></div>
